--- END SUMMARY ---
HITLS HRV: Heart-Rate Variability Analysis
================================================================================
Input signal      : RR intervals (ms) from Polar H10 / Elite HRV export
Synchronization   : eliteHRV app elapsed time aligned to ingescape unix timestamps
Scenario slicing  : per scenario from scenario ingescape start/end timestamps
Procedure slicing : procedure transitions from current_state (fallback: force_state_jump)
Domains computed  : NeuroKit2 HRV all domains (time, frequency, non-linear)
Dynamic workload  : RMSSD on 60 s windows (step 30 s) + per-procedure RMSSD
Statistics        : Friedman test + pairwise Wilcoxon (Holm correction)
================================================================================

Participants with HRV data (n=11): P02, P03, P04, P05, P06, P07, P08, P09, P10, P12, P13

Why these calculations:
  • Time-domain metrics (RMSSD, SDNN, MeanNN, pNN50) quantify short- and global variability
    linked to mental effort and stress reactivity.
  • Frequency-domain metrics (LF, HF, LF/HF, LFNU, HFNU) characterize spectral balance
    between autonomic components across conditions.
  • Non-linear metrics (SD1/SD2, DFA alpha1, SampEn) capture complexity changes
    not visible in linear summaries.
  • Repeated-measures non-parametric tests are used because data are paired by participant
    and normality cannot be assumed.
